# Freshers School Life — voice recording script

Generated by `tools/voice/make_script.js` from the story scripts (`docs/story/scripts/`) and the game's own lines. **18 voice roles, 5242 recordings of 4479 different lines** (tier A 1605, B 2748, C 889). Each role has its own hand-out PDF in `docs/voice/pdf/` with the instructions inside; this file is the plain list.

| Role | Who | Lines (A/B/C) | Minutes (A / all) |

| The Maker (owner) | Muhammad Iliyasu Bugaje himself. The owner records this one. | 247 (117/114/16) | 23 / 51 |

Record in order, Tier A first. Do not read the numbers aloud. Leave about a second and a half of silence between lines. If you fluff a line, clap once and say it again. Save as `<role>_<yourname>.m4a` (any audio format) and drop it in `voice_in/`.

## The Player (male) — `player_m`

**Who:** A young man, 17 to 22. The owner records this one. **Characters:** The student you play, when the player picks a male character.

**Who:** Muhammad Iliyasu Bugaje himself. The owner records this one. **Characters:** The Maker, Young Maker, Maker

### Tier A · The Maker's sayings

*After the ring is lit, the Maker walks the campus free. Talking to him gives one saying. Dry, kind and exact; a smile you can hear.*

| No. | Say this | Who / how | id |
|---|---|---|---|
| 74 | Energy in, light out. The rest is commentary. | a saying | `v90c12e2e16` |
| 75 | A thing is finished when it no longer needs you. | a saying | `v224dc64d1b` |
| 76 | I kept one light on for a long time. You taught me to share the switch. | a saying | `v068b909d1x` |
| 77 | Nobody lights a ring alone. I checked. For years. | a saying | `vc7af78be1a` |
| 78 | The dark is not an enemy. It is what is there before somebody bothers. | a saying | `v81a1552b1w` |
| 79 | Start small. A torch has never been embarrassed by the sun. | a saying | `vc93c54e61l` |
| 80 | If you are tired, rest. If you are stuck, move. Learn which one it is. | a saying | `v02aeb0461t` |
| 81 | The best machine I ever built was a queue of people passing parts. | a saying | `v20a7da781t` |
| 82 | Read the manual. Then read the person. | a saying | `v971dee8b10` |
| 83 | You will forget the grade. You will remember who lent you a pen. | a saying | `vc3b6f1cd1q` |
| 84 | Every expert was once the one asking where the Senate is. | a saying | `v1c6867fe1k` |
| 85 | Measure twice. Then ask someone to measure. Then cut. | a saying | `v1421a5cf1e` |
| 86 | A carry-over is a second lecture you paid for with pride. | a saying | `vcd7fddee1k` |
| 87 | Money is stored effort. Spend it on what stores well: skills, friends, shoes. | a saying | `v5a0600b720` |
| 88 | The loudest person in the room is usually running on low battery. | a saying | `vf01c191e1s` |
| 89 | Be the reason the fresher finds the hall. | a saying | `v164ae0e914` |
| 90 | I used to think staying was loyalty. Sometimes leaving is how you keep faith. | a saying | `v7be4da2a23` |
| 91 | Light travels fast. Kindness travels further. | a saying | `v68dd21f617` |
| 92 | When they take light, sit near people. That is the whole trick. | a saying | `vc2d563851o` |
| 93 | Entropy always wins in the end. So enjoy the middle and tidy as you go. | a saying | `v0eed90ac1x` |
| 94 | A good question is worth two answers and one cup of tea. | a saying | `v16596c101j` |
| 95 | Do not confuse being seen with being known. | a saying | `v1a10edfe16` |
| 96 | The coil hums at one note. Find yours. It need not be loud. | a saying | `vb35293fd1k` |
| 97 | Borrow tools. Return them cleaner. This is most of ethics. | a saying | `v23fdd51b1j` |
| 98 | Your friends are your backup generator. Service them regularly. | a saying | `vbb47681d1p` |
| 99 | The shortest path is rarely the one with the best suya. | a saying | `v4d0434f01i` |
| 100 | I have watched forty first days. Every one of them looked like fear and was actually hope. | a saying | `v25eddbd52g` |
| 101 | Garri week comes to us all. Share the cup. | a saying | `va0999f8514` |
| 102 | A lecture is someone handing you ten years in fifty minutes. Sit up. | a saying | `v4f21d4fe1u` |
| 103 | Whatever you practise at night is who you are by morning. | a saying | `v4b5d984a1k` |
| 104 | Fame is a lamp pointed at you. Useful is a lamp you hold. | a saying | `va15d2e4f1j` |
| 105 | Greet the porter. He was here before you and will be here after. | a saying | `v70353e331q` |
| 106 | You cannot solder in a hurry. The same is true of trust. | a saying | `vfce5bd001i` |
| 107 | If the plan needs a hero, it is a bad plan. Draw it again with a team. | a saying | `v879d48d71v` |
| 108 | The school is not the buildings. I learned this late. Learn it early. | a saying | `vecc625c11u` |
| 109 | It is allowed to be proud of something small. | a saying | `v5617d51a18` |
| 110 | Call home. They do not need news. They need your voice. | a saying | `v65eb93801g` |
| 111 | The exam ends. The understanding is yours to keep. | a saying | `v9f71066c1c` |
| 112 | When you leave, leave something switched on. | a saying | `v85a1c68c16` |
| 113 | I was a student here for longer than was sensible. I recommend about five years. | a saying | `v2ba5f7ac26` |
| 114 | Stand where the fresher can see you. That is what seniors are for. | a saying | `v6217175d1s` |
| 115 | Some days the bravest thing is to open the door and go to class. | a saying | `v1e9abaa81r` |
| 116 | A switch has two positions. People have more. Be patient with them. | a saying | `v586009e81s` |
| 117 | Thank you. I do not say it enough, and I had a lot of time to practise. | a saying | `vf61d301e1w` |

### Tier C · The six plaques in the Engineering Garden

*Each plaque is read aloud when the player stands at it. Read like a guide in a museum, slowly. These are long: one breath per sentence is fine, but keep every pause under half a second.*

| No. | Say this | Who / how | id |
|---|---|---|---|
| 242 | The coil above you is his: a resonant transformer he built in 1891 that turns ordinary voltage into hundreds of thousands of volts and throws it into the air as sparks. Tesla designed the AC induction motor and the polyphase system that still carries power across whole countries. The unit of magnetic flux density, the tesla, carries his name. | plaque: Nikola Tesla | `vc5e932aa9e` |
| 243 | A bookbinder's apprentice with almost no schooling. In 1831 he showed that a changing magnetic field drives a current: electromagnetic induction. Every generator and every transformer on this campus works because of that one experiment. The farad, the unit of capacitance, is named after him. | plaque: Michael Faraday | `v39d0206e7x` |
| 244 | He wrote the equations that tie electricity, magnetism and light together, and predicted that light itself is an electromagnetic wave. Radio, radar, Wi-Fi and the phone in your pocket all follow from those equations. | plaque: James Clerk Maxwell | `vae1076225u` |
| 245 | A schoolteacher who measured, patiently, how current depends on voltage and resistance, and published V = IR in 1827. His own colleagues dismissed it at first. It is now the first line of every circuits course, including yours. | plaque: Georg Simon Ohm | `vfef5472b62` |
| 246 | Within weeks of hearing that a current deflects a compass, he worked out the force between two current-carrying wires and founded electrodynamics. The ampere, the unit of current, honours him. | plaque: André-Marie Ampère | `v792a2e6357` |
| 247 | The engineer behind everything you are standing in. M.I. BUGAJE built EE Hub, the department's whole online home, and then built Freshers School Life on top of it: this campus, these roads, the classes, the okadas, the shades and the story. The scientists on the other plaques explained how the world works. He used it to build a world. If you are enjoying the game, find him and say thank you. | plaque: Muhammad Iliyasu Bugaje | `v08da366dak` |
